# Add 57 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 57 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native TRS-80 utilities synchronising TRSDOS/LS-DOS date and time with supported emulator host-clock ports.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Classification (reviewed)

Four native clock utilities implement US/European date formats, emulator IN-port access, LS-DOS fallback manual entry and cached-date checksum. This is distinct system utility source, not an emulator itself. Root GPL-3.0 text.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native Z80 assembly establishes source architecture; no minimum runtime machine is inferred.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native implementation/build establishes Z80 output architecture; no complete minimum hardware configuration is inferred.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Build (reviewed)

JCL scripts invoke MRAS (and MLINK for TRSDOS variants) inside a configured TRS-80 environment. Some CMD binaries are present; host import, disk drives and emulator extension setup are external. No upstream software was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

README specifies TRSDOS 6.x/LS-DOS 6.3.1L and emulator ports 68H–6DH; source explicitly targets Model 4 with Keil extensions. Exact cross-emulator compatibility and RAM minimum were not tested.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### AI (no-evidence-found)

Unknown: bounded source/documentation and pinned latest-commit inspection found no explicit AI contribution attribution; non-use is not inferred.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CLKLSDOS/blob/97fe82da22b224a81e811317a15031e3f1f37a7e/CLKLSDOS/CLKLD63X.ASM

### Identity (reviewed)

Original author’s recovered mid-1980s TRS-80 Model I cassette BASIC extension source with extended graphics, music, strings and program editing.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/XBASIC/blob/ad16ba89b04a55e065e40ba8b6e189dd189a07f1/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/XBASIC/blob/ad16ba89b04a55e065e40ba8b6e189dd189a07f1/XBASIC/XBASIC.ASM

### Classification (reviewed)

Author explicitly says source was recovered from cassette in six files and merged into one assembly file. Actual extension vectors, DRAW algorithm, PLAY parser, dynamic execution and renumbering routines substantiate original-source preservation. Version remains 0.0.1-alpha and author warns of bugs/error-checking gaps. Root GPL-3.0 text; author identifies this as own recovered work, not recovered Microsoft BASIC source.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/XBASIC/blob/ad16ba89b04a55e065e40ba8b6e189dd189a07f1/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/XBASIC/blob/ad16ba89b04a55e065e40ba8b6e189dd189a07f1/XBASIC/XBASIC.ASM

### Build (reviewed)

Develop-branch Windows batch invokes external ZMAC --zmac with -P0=4 to emit 500-baud CAS/LST/BDS and rename CAS. It then calls absent run_xbasic.bat; build and launch must be distinguished. Original Level II ROM and emulator/hardware are external. No upstream software was compiled or executed. Root BSD-2-Clause; inspected files also include public-domain MED comments and cpu_control.c commercial-permission restrictions. These mixed per-file notices prevent an uncomplicated repository-wide licence assertion.

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected Ramsey addressing tables, bank-to-address conversion, data/address-line tests and actual 68k RAM test assembly. Running from chip RAM and interrupt suppression are correctness requirements. Author docs speculate about A4000 applicability, but source warns other models may crash; catalogue scope is A3000/A3000T. BSD-2-Clause root LICENSE conflicts with ziptest.c header requiring permission for commercial use; preserve both rather than declare blanket permissive rights.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original BFFS AmigaOS filesystem source release with read/write BSD UFS/FFS support and native filesystem-checking, formatting, dump/restore and inspection utilities.
- https://github.com/cdhooper/bffs/blob/949061ab5635953f60751a442482c29f0e45750a/dist/bffs16_src.readme

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected DOS packet handlers for locate/examine/read/write/seek and UFS partition/superblock/endian handling. Author documents UFS V1 read/write but UFS V2 read-only at best; do not advertise full UFS2. Source first released with 1.6, an update to the 1996 1.5 release. Current inspected source/build files use permissive retain-notice terms, historical doc/legal_information says freeware/no modified redistribution, and NetBSD-derived utility/header components retain upstream conditions. Mixed periods/components require individual review.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native bouncing-ball/paddle demonstration built to exercise a real cooperative/preemptive context-switching library, mutexes and ring-buffer communication. Each ball is a separate task, with architecture-specific SM83 and Z80 register-save/restore code; it is development research rather than a claimed full Arkanoid reconstruction.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Game Boy 2.5D/isometric room engine and interactive demonstration with 9×9×4-cell scenes, room/world linked-list maps, collision reconstruction, masked rendering, rotation, shadows and enemy movement. Includes project-specific scene/map and GBTD converters rather than just a published ROM.

- https://github.com/untoxa/ISOEngine/blob/c466cc164414935ce7916506cf7629ebe0d308fb/Makefile

### Identity (reviewed)

Open-source native Game Boy interface ROM for the Orange FM custom radio cartridge, implementing frequency/preset/volume/settings UI, radio/RDS/status synchronization and a cartridge-MCU command protocol. Interrupt-driven sleeping minimizes console interference with radio reception.
- https://github.com/orangeglo/orange-fm-rom/blob/08f06daaf974fec82c60ec8a35976c0e86986b0a/README.md
- https://github.com/orangeglo/orange-fm-rom/blob/08f06daaf974fec82c60ec8a35976c0e86986b0a/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Production build explicitly requires Orange FM circuit-board hardware. The mock build cannot establish physical FM reception; it is an emulator-compatible protocol stand-in, not a different historical CPU emulator. DMG/CGB/SGB header support is present. Root MIT licence inspected; external material retains its own provenance.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Game Boy/Game Boy Color authenticator implementing HMAC-SHA1/RFC6238-style TOTP, base32 account entry, MBC3 RTC access and battery-SRAM storage, plus GB-specific UI/audio/SGB border. Pure-C crypto and concrete hardware glue are present, independently of its sibling GBA port.
- https://github.com/dmang-dev/totp-gb/blob/d088fc1e4676cb7424e0e0fc10b59a54ecce92ee/README.md

Author claims 8 accounts and live 30-second codes. Security, timekeeping, secret storage and RFC conformance have not been independently audited; this catalogue inclusion is not an endorsement for real account protection. Test accounts/fixtures were not used to access any service. Root MIT licence inspected; external material retains its own provenance.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original CGB-only Mobile Adapter GB diagnostic client with separate native GBDK/C and RGBDS/SM83 implementations of packet framing, checksum/session/ACK handling, adapter config readout and network tests. It exercises actual serial protocol behavior rather than repackaging a Pokémon ROM or wrapping a guest CPU.
- https://github.com/zenaror/MAGB-TestSuite/blob/7be0236ab7faad6bf692ff9a6b59ff37312343ab/README.md
- https://github.com/zenaror/MAGB-TestSuite/blob/7be0236ab7faad6bf692ff9a6b59ff37312343ab/gbdk/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Author reports GBDK real-hardware coverage; RGBDS remains in progress with status-documented gaps and unimplemented network submenu items. Config substructures beyond first ISP slot are incompletely decoded. Protocol implementation and tests are useful at this demonstrated partial scope. Root GPL-3.0 licence inspected; README states original homebrew without copyrighted Nintendo game assets.
- https://github.com/zenaror/MAGB-TestSuite/blob/7be0236ab7faad6bf692ff9a6b59ff37312343ab/README.md
- https://github.com/zenaror/MAGB-TestSuite/blob/7be0236ab7faad6bf692ff9a6b59ff37312343ab/gbdk/Makefile

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Game Boy 4×4×4 tic-tac-toe with 76-line board evaluation, computer move/fork scoring, three difficulty levels and rotatable sliced-board UI. Author traces own implementation from ZX81 Sinclair BASIC circa 1982 through QL/BBC BASIC and a 2004 C version, then a GBDK UI; predecessor binaries are not analyzed here.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved Samuel Audet 1997 DOS utility that probes Sound Blaster access and rewrites Win-OS/2 SYSTEM.INI driver lines to avoid modal errors when the sound card is unavailable.
- https://github.com/OS2World/WIN16-WinSB/blob/a6800bc7d1183fe47a165661f7886bccf6c91dab/README.md
- https://github.com/OS2World/WIN16-WinSB/blob/a6800bc7d1183fe47a165661f7886bccf6c91dab/readme.txt

### Classification (reviewed)

Repository prefix WIN16 does not mean the program is a Win16 executable: source is a DOS Pascal helper for Win-OS/2 sessions. Actual DSP reset loop, INI parser and driver-comment update are present. SYSTEM.BAK only preserves the previous run; author warns of destructive overwrite risk and Warp3 sound-card bugs. Root says licence not specified; source says distribute freely. This is not a broad modification licence. Original author/copyright retained.
- https://github.com/OS2World/WIN16-WinSB/blob/a6800bc7d1183fe47a165661f7886bccf6c91dab/README.md

John McSorley 1995 PC Magazine Win16 system utility that allocates conventional-memory blocks and shrinks them to influence low-memory allocation, with companion Visual Basic memory viewer.

- https://github.com/OS2World/WIN16-1MBFort/blob/ed45e698279650549fb9bb26569ef7018b267bd6/READ.ME

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual GlobalDosAlloc loop and GlobalReAlloc shrink-to-one-byte logic inspected, beyond generic WinMain scaffolding. Preserve as historical workaround source, not tested evidence that it repairs all insufficient-memory failures. /G2 is compiler generation evidence only. No licence specified by repository. C copyright identifies John McSorley and PC Magazine first publication on 1995-03-28; preservation is not a verified open licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Jason Hood SHSUCD suite derived from John McCoy SHSUCD: MSCDEX-compatible redirector, ISO-backed CD/DVD drivers, optical-image creation and El Torito boot-image extraction.

### Classification (reviewed)

Real INT2F redirector/ISO9660 implementation and El Torito parser inspected. Keep suite together, including small SMARTDrive patcher, rather than inflate individual utilities. CD drive driver or ISO input required; author source calls rewrite from prior NASM version. Jason Hood LICENSE uses zlib-style permission with origin/alteration/notice restrictions. Retain predecessor John McCoy copyright/free-use notice and separate zlib dependency licensing rather than asserting all artifacts share a grant.

Imre Leber DOS CHKDSK reimplementation with FAT12/16 image and disk checks, cross-link/lost-cluster repair, directory and long-name checking, surface scan and XMS sector cache.

Inspected real volume check/fix sequencing and cross-linked-cluster data structures, distinct from already tracked FreeDOS DEFRAG despite shared FTE lineage. Documentation limits support to FAT12/16 with512-byte sectors and says boot errors cannot be repaired. Version documentation contains beta0.9 and0.31 text; no inferred modern FAT32 compatibility. Inspected source GPLv2-or-later; original disklib credit remains in documentation.

C implementation of the Medley Interlisp bytecode virtual machine, garbage collection and host display/filesystem/network interfaces, preserving and extending the historical Venue runtime.

Virtual-machine runtime is separate from the Lisp environment in Interlisp/medley. No fixed original physical CPU applies to this bytecode implementation. MIT LICENSE; NOTICE explicitly thanks Venue rights inheritor Wayne Booth Marci for permission to release the historical code under MIT. Older source copyright notices retained.
- https://github.com/Interlisp/maiko/blob/76e7850ac8959526b7be07ebd4e1aab892d80df3/README.md

Original runtime implements the byte-coded Interlisp instruction set, not a single physical host architecture; hardware source_cpu is not applicable. README lists tested host architectures without establishing an original CPU.

PDP-1 is documented as the most polished and PDP-5 as little tested. Remote debugger source contains breakpoints, watchpoints and bounded trace/run handling. One repository-wide record keeps shared infrastructure together. MIT root license. GPIO/pinctrl and other bundled dependencies retain their own notices. Historical program rights are separate.

Modern .NET Xerox Alto I/II XM emulator with microcode engine, Diablo/Trident disks, Ethernet, display/input, print raster hardware and integrated debugger.

Author all-known-software claim remains attributed, with serial/IMP/exotic hardware not supported. BSD-3-Clause root and CPU source. The older ContrAlto repository has an AGPLv3 licence text, so no claim is made that both snapshots have identical licensing. Bundled historical ROM/disk rights are separate.

### Identity (reviewed)

Java reimplementation of Xerox Network Services including Courier/RPC, clearinghouse/authentication, boot, filing, printing, mail, external communication and virtual Ethernet hub.
- https://github.com/devhawala/dodo/blob/83739e468e85a457ae1cd40ddc4de5536c2f0b16/readme.md
- https://github.com/devhawala/dodo/blob/83739e468e85a457ae1cd40ddc4de5536c2f0b16/src/dev/hawala/xns/level4/filing/FilingImpl.java

### Classification (reviewed)

Material protocol reconstruction enabling real/emulated historical workstations. Several services implement useful subsets, filing lacks access-control support and clearinghouse is read-only; not production security software. BSD-3-Clause root and source headers; externally supplied Xerox boot files/microcode require their own rights review.
- https://github.com/devhawala/dodo/blob/83739e468e85a457ae1cd40ddc4de5536c2f0b16/readme.md
- https://github.com/devhawala/dodo/blob/83739e468e85a457ae1cd40ddc4de5536c2f0b16/src/dev/hawala/xns/level4/filing/FilingImpl.java

## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
